- **hi-c-fastq-to-contact-map-pipeline**: Use when you have raw Hi-C FASTQ files from a sequencing experiment and need to generate kilobase-resolution Hi-C contact maps conforming to ENCODE reference standards. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/epigenomics/v1/skills/hi-c-fastq-to-contact-map-pipeline))
- **hi-c-input-table-preprocessing**: Use when raw Hi-C or pcHi-C feature tables (e.g. GM19204.chr17.cis.feature.gz with columns frag1, frag2, cov_frag1, cov_frag2, freq, dist) must be cleaned before normalization or reproducibility scoring. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/epigenomics/v2/leaves/hi-c-input-table-preprocessing))
- **hi-c-insulation-score-calculation**: Use when you have a cooler-format Hi-C contact matrix and need to identify TAD boundaries and insulation strength along the genome. Use this skill when your research question requires quantifying local chromatin compartmentalization or annotating structural domain edges for downstream analysis (e. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/epigenomics/v1/skills/hi-c-insulation-score-calculation))
- **hi-c-interaction-modeling**: Use when fitting a generative model of Hi-C interaction counts (e.g. CscoreTool-M) where expected counts Fij must incorporate Rabl-position effects, bias factors, and per-window sub-compartment probabilities, and the likelihood of observed trans reads is needed. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/epigenomics/v2/leaves/hi-c-interaction-modeling))
- **hi-c-loop-calling-benchmarking**: Use when comparing a Hi-C loop caller (e.g., GILoop) against machine-learning baselines like Peakachu on matched contact maps, requiring PR-AUC, APA enrichment, and loop-recovery evaluation against a ground-truth loop set. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/epigenomics/v2/leaves/hi-c-loop-calling-benchmarking))
- **hi-c-map-artifact-validation**: Use when after executing the Juicer pipeline on raw Hi-C FASTQ files, to confirm that the pipeline has generated the expected .hic output artifact and that the contact matrix construction and normalization steps completed without error. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/epigenomics/v1/skills/hi-c-map-artifact-validation))
- **hi-c-matrix-normalization-pipeline-setup**: Use when before running HiC-Pro's normalization stage on aligned Hi-C BAM files. Specifically, when you have SAM/BAM-formatted aligned Hi-C reads that need bias correction and matrix balancing to produce normalized contact maps suitable for downstream chromatin structure analysis. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/epigenomics/v1/skills/hi-c-matrix-normalization-pipeline-setup))
- **hi-c-normalization-and-bias-correction**: Use when after generating raw Hi-C contact matrices from aligned reads (post-merge, pre-analysis). ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/epigenomics/v1/skills/hi-c-normalization-and-bias-correction))
- **hi-c-reproducibility-scoring-hic-spector**: Use when you have cis-interaction contact maps of two biological replicates (e.g., 40 kb-resolution chr17 maps) and need to quantify and compare how well normalization methods (covNorm, ICE, KR, SCN) improve replicate reproducibility. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/epigenomics/v2/leaves/hi-c-reproducibility-scoring-hic-spector))
- **hi-c-significant-contact-calling**: Use when you have covNorm distance-normalized contact tables (frag1/frag2, cov, freq, dist) and need p-value/FDR-based significant long-range interaction calling. - **hi-c-trans-interaction-simulation-from-compartment-model**: Use when validating that a sub-compartment inference method recovers known compartment scores from simulated Hi-C trans-interaction data, or when testing how low sequencing depth can go before inference degrades.
